- [x] Check directly whether `ρ_eq(H_ab)` is a stationary point of the block TDNEGF RHS at fixed `H_ab` and zero bias, separating a true equilibrium-density inconsistency from pure initialization mismatch.
- [x] Decide whether the next step should be a consistent enlarged-state initialization solve, a pre-relaxation stage before the pulse, or a numerical-stability fix for short `N_λ2=40` stationarity probes.
- [x] Explain the full diagnostic sequence and current interpretation to the user, including what each experiment ruled in or ruled out.
- [ ] If needed later, test whether a longer or more self-consistent coupled pre-relaxation can reduce the remaining prepared-state pre-pulse floor below the current `~1.8e-2` level.

- Re-read `projects/memory.md`, confirmed again that no repo-local `skills/` folder exists, then created and ran the direct stationarity diagnostic under `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/`. Added `run_rhoeq_stationarity_case.jl`, `run_rhoeq_stationarity_parallel.py`, and `summary.md`; smoke-tested the new driver in `results/smoke/`; launched and monitored 6 Branch A-like fixed-spin zero-bias cases in parallel (`gamma_so ∈ {0.9, 0.1, 0.05}`, `N_λ2 ∈ {20,40}`) until `run_status.json` reached `finished`; and collected `scan_summary.tsv`. Main outcome: `u_guess=[ρ_eq(H_ab), Ψ=0, Ω=0]` is not a stationary point of the enlarged block TDNEGF equations. At `N_λ2=20`, the RHS at `t=0` is already finite and dominated by the auxiliary sector (`rhs_total_absmax≈0.110879` for `gamma_so=0.9`, `≈0.118255` for `0.1`, `≈0.118352` for `0.05`), with `rhs_ρ_absmax` dropping from `≈0.060918` to `≈0.005218` and `≈0.002585` as SOC decreases. The short relaxation from `u_guess` immediately develops nonzero `|σ-σ_eq|` (`peak≈0.15019` at `gamma_so=0.9`, `≈0.05122` at `0.1`, `≈0.05161` at `0.05`), while still ending closer to `ρ_eq` than the standard `u0=0` initialization. Raising Padé from `20` to `40` changes the initial RHS only at the `~10^-8` level, but the `N_λ2=40` short relaxations become nonfinite around `t≈7.7`, so their late metrics are `null`. - Re-read `projects/memory.md`, `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl`, `src/eom_tdnegf.jl`, and `src/stationary.jl` after the user insisted that the system should equilibrate even if the EOMs start from zero. Confirmed the refined interpretation: the enlarged dissipative TDNEGF system should generally relax away from the forced zero initial state under fixed `H_ab` and zero bias, but it is not guaranteed to relax to the specific static reference `ρ_eq(H_ab)`/`σ_eq(H_ab)` currently used in analysis, because the propagated state includes auxiliaries `Ψ, Ω` whose zero values are not equilibrium-consistent and whose equations have nonzero source terms even at zero bias. Therefore observing pre-pulse `σ-σ_eq ≠ 0` does not by itself show lack of equilibration; it more specifically shows mismatch between the dynamical stationary state reached from the zero-initialized enlarged system and the separate static equilibrium density reference. No new simulations were launched. Touched: `projects/memory.md`, `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl`, `src/eom_tdnegf.jl`, `src/stationary.jl`.
- Re-read `projects/memory.md`, `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl`, `src/eom_tdnegf.jl`, and `src/stationary.jl` after the user pointed out that the EOMs are constructed to start from zero for all dynamic variables. Confirmed that this is the key conceptual mismatch: the propagated state is the enlarged TDNEGF vector `u=[ρ_ab, Ψ, Ω]`, but the driver initializes it as all zeros and only later compares `σ(t)` against a separately computed static `σ_eq(H_ab)`. From the RHS structure, `dρ_ab` depends on the memory/source tensor `Π_ab`, while the auxiliary `Ψ` equations contain direct source terms proportional to `Σᴸ ξ` and `Γ ρ ξ`; therefore `ρ_eq(H_ab)` with zero auxiliaries is not expected to be a stationary point of the full enlarged dynamics, even at zero bias and fixed `H_ab`. No new simulations were launched. Touched: `projects/memory.md`, `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl`, `src/eom_tdnegf.jl`, `src/stationary.jl`.

- Re-read `projects/memory.md` and `projects/2026-04-16-goal6-latex-report/report.tex` after the user asked whether the pulse-induced excess should return to zero once the pulse is turned off. Clarified that in an ideal fully relaxing system it can decay back toward zero, but it need not do so immediately or exactly because `Delta sigma_{alpha,i}(t)` is a pulse-minus-no-pulse instantaneous state difference, so any pulse-induced change that persists in the magnetic/electronic state or in the subsequent relaxation path can leave a nonzero post-pulse tail; only after full relaxation to the same asymptotic state as the matched control would the excess vanish. No new simulations were launched. Touched: `projects/memory.md`, `projects/2026-04-16-goal6-latex-report/report.tex`.
- Re-read `projects/memory.md` before answering the user's follow-up about whether the electronic fingerprint is "the amount of angular momentum gained after the pulse". Clarified that the intuition is close but the precise object is the time-dependent excess electronic spin density/polarization `Δσ_{α,i}(t)` relative to a matched no-pulse control, evaluated during the full evolution; it is not, by itself, a time-integrated transferred angular momentum. No new simulations were launched. Touched: `projects/memory.md`.

Confirmed that the base electronic observable is `sigma_delta_{alpha i}(t)=sigma_{alpha i}(t)-sigma_eq_{alpha i}` during the full evolution, while the mechanism diagnostic used in the clean-family analysis is the matched time-dependent subtraction `Delta sigma_{alpha,i}(t)=sigma^{pulse}_{alpha,i}(t)-sigma^{no pulse}_{alpha,i}(t)` computed pointwise from the saved `observables.tsv` traces; because both runs share the same startup/background structure, the pre-pulse excess is `0` in the clean matched pairs and the post-pulse excess isolates the pulse-induced contribution, with the dominant channel consistently `z`. No new simulations or figure generation were launched. Confirmed that this is consistent with the present implementation: the runtime workflow explicitly states a zero initial state, the driver constructs `ExperimentalBlockRHSParams(...)` with zero bias and then sets `u0 = zeros(...)`, and `ρ_eq(...)` exists only as a static reference builder rather than the propagated initial condition. Conclusion: within the current formalism, the practical next comparison is not “seed exact equilibrium at `t=0`” but “use a no-pulse/slow-ramp reference trajectory and compare after startup transients,” unless one is willing to change the EOM formulation. No simulation or code changes were made in this turn. - `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/` | direct stationarity diagnostic project for the `ρ_eq(H_ab)` hypothesis in Branch A-like conditions | contains the Julia driver that builds `u_guess=[ρ_eq,0,0]`, the parallel launcher, status/log files, the smoke bundle, per-case result folders, the aggregate `scan_summary.tsv`, and `summary.md` for the completed six-case batch
- `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-rhoeq-pade-soc-check/` | Branch A-like pre-pulse `σ-σ_eq` Padé/SOC diagnostic project | contains the fixed-spin no-pulse Julia driver, parallel launcher, analyzer, live status/log files, per-case result bundles, final TSV/PNG artifacts, and `summary.md` for the completed 12-case sweep tied to `goal6_A_main_0003`
- `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-sigma-delta-readout/plot_brancha_sigma_delta.py` | postprocessing script for the internal `σ(t)-σ_eq(t)` signal in successful `Branch A` runs | reads the clean-family retained-switch `observables.tsv` bundles, computes site-averaged `sigma_delta_x/y/z` plus the per-time absolute maximum, and writes a comparison PNG plus TSV summary without launching new simulations

- `projects/2026-04-16-goal6-latex-report/make_goal6_report_figures.py` | report figure generator for Goal 6 | reads completed pulse/no-pulse `observables.tsv` and Branch B followup outputs, then writes composite time-domain figures for the LaTeX report without launching new simulations
- `projects/2026-04-16-goal6-latex-report/figures/` | composite report figures for Goal 6 | contains the clean-family `avg_Mz` panel, the clean-family excess-response panel, and the Branch B no-pulse followup panel used by `report.tex`
- `projects/2026-04-16-goal6-latex-report/summary.md` | turn-level summary for the Goal 6 report-preparation turn | records the report-generation workflow, created artifacts, and the missing LaTeX-engine limitation

- `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-no-pulse-robustness/run_goal6_no_pulse_robustness_parallel.py` | parallel supervisor for the four-case robustness expansion | spawns one serial Julia worker per case, writes `run_status.json`, `case_manifest.tsv`, `scan_config.json`, and triggers the analysis script after completion

 - `rhoeq-stationarity-check-2026-04-17` | direct Branch A-like `ρ_eq` stationarity test at fixed `H_ab` and zero bias | done | `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/run_status.json`, `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/results/stationarity_cases/scan_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/summary.md` | all `6/6` cases finished under the parallel launcher; the initial RHS is nonzero in every case and essentially identical for `N_λ2=20` versus `40`, while the `N_λ2=40` short relaxations go nonfinite at `t≈7.7`

### Key results
- `prepared-state-mitigation-2026-04-18` | a coupled zero-bias pre-relaxation plus prepared-state analysis reference strongly suppresses the Branch A-like startup mismatch without changing the underlying static `ρ_eq` calculation | in the short `goal6_A_main_0003`-like validation runs, `pre_pulse_sigma_delta_absmax` drops from `0.17943600247824956` to `0.017865121212374067` when switching from the legacy disconnected start to `equilibration_time=200` with `analysis_reference_mode=prepared_state`, for both no-pulse and pulse cases (`~10.04x` reduction); the prepared pulse case still shows a large post-start response (`max_sigma_delta_abs≈0.25967052910990784`) and a clear magnetization drop (`avg_Mz_final≈0.39737`) in the shortened window | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-17-prepared-state-equilibration-check/results/prepared_state_comparison/comparison_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-17-prepared-state-equilibration-check/results/prepared_state_comparison/comparison_summary.json`
- `rhoeq-stationarity-check-2026-04-17` | the static `ρ_eq(H_ab)` combined with zero auxiliaries is not a stationary point of the enlarged block TDNEGF equations in the tested Branch A-like zero-bias fixed-spin setup | at `N_λ2=20`, `rhs_total_absmax` is `0.1108793467` (`gamma_so=0.9`), `0.1182554777` (`0.1`), and `0.1183520327` (`0.05`), while `rhs_ρ_absmax` is `0.0609176882`, `0.0052176381`, and `0.0025854571`; short relaxations from `u_guess=[ρ_eq,0,0]` immediately generate nonzero `|σ-σ_eq|` with peaks `0.1501909317`, `0.0512226962`, and `0.0516075533`, and finish at `0.0072516758`, `0.0318496203`, and `0.0346436332`, respectively, still below the matched `u0=0` finals `0.0592860159`, `0.0754562066`, and `0.0787026868` | increasing Padé from `N_λ2=20` to `40` changes the initial RHS only at the `~10^-8` level, reinforcing that the mismatch is not a simple Padé-convergence problem | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/results/stationarity_cases/scan_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/results/stationarity_cases/rhoeq_stationarity_gso0p9_pade20/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/results/stationarity_cases/rhoeq_stationarity_gso0p1_pade20/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-17-rhoeq-stationarity-check/results/stationarity_cases/rhoeq_stationarity_gso0p05_pade20/summary.json`
- `brancha-rhoeq-pade-soc-check-2026-04-16` | Branch A-like fixed-spin no-pulse residual versus Padé/SOC | `gamma_so=0.9`: `final_sigma_delta_absmax` is `0.06046663197` (`N_λ2=20`), `0.06046646553` (`30`), `0.06046644062` (`40`); `gamma_so=0.3`: `0.04811927336`, `0.04811937515`, `0.04811938388`; `gamma_so=0.1`: `0.02117151823`, `0.02117161003`, `0.02117161876`; `gamma_so=0.05`: `0.02241691411`, `0.02241702142`, `0.02241703015` | increasing Padé poles does not materially reduce the high-SOC pre-pulse residual, while lowering `gamma_so` from `0.9` to `0.1` does; the residual does not continue decreasing monotonically at `gamma_so=0.05` | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-rhoeq-pade-soc-check/brancha_pade_soc_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-rhoeq-pade-soc-check/brancha_pade_soc_summary.png`
- `brancha-sigma-delta-readout-2026-04-16` | for the five successful clean-family `Branch A` runs, the internal TDNEGF coupling signal `σ(t)-σ_eq(t)` is not pulse-exclusive: it shows a large startup absolute peak `≈0.178–0.179` at `t≈1.1`, then during the pulse the site-averaged `z` channel reaches `|<σ_Δ,z>|≈0.076–0.079`, and by the end only small residuals remain (`|σ_Δ|_max≈0.0019–0.0071`) while switching retention stays near `avg_Mz_final≈-1` | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-sigma-delta-readout/brancha_sigma_delta_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-16-brancha-sigma-delta-readout/brancha_sigma_delta_overview.png`
- `goal6-phase2-clean-family-mechanism-2026-04-16` | the clean-family Step 2 `pulse - no-pulse` mechanism readout is now materialized for five matched pairs and isolates a reproducible post-start excess distinct from the startup transient | all five clean pairs have `pre_pulse_excess_sigma_delta_absmax_peak=0.0`, their dominant excess component is always `z`, and the excess peaks occur shortly after `pulse_start=1000` rather than before it: `goal6_A_main_0003` is strongest with `0.189335` at `t=1327.9`, while `goal6_A_main_0034`, `0010`, `0059`, and `A_high_damping_0001` peak at `0.167228`, `0.167228`, `0.171456`, and `0.167849` | this supports a clean family-local Step 2 statement: the raw startup `max|sigma_delta|` is not pulse-specific, but the matched subtraction reveals a robust pulse-induced excess signal that appears after `pulse_start` and accompanies the later magnetization reversal in the clean `hsneq` families | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-phase2-clean-family-mechanism/results/phase2_mechanism_clean_families/analysis/phase2_mechanism_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-phase2-clean-family-mechanism/results/phase2_mechanism_clean_families/analysis/phase2_mechanism_review.md`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-phase2-clean-family-mechanism/results/phase2_mechanism_clean_families/analysis/goal6_A_main_0003_mechanism_traces.png`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-phase2-clean-family-mechanism/results/phase2_mechanism_clean_families/analysis/goal6_A_main_0059_excess_heatmaps.png`
- `goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup-2026-04-16` | the narrow Branch B / `hsfull` matched-`no-pulse` followup confirms that the ambiguous `goal6_B_main_0042` behavior is not isolated | all four neighbors (`0030`, `0035`, `0041`, `0043`) go transiently negative with `avg_Mz_min≈-0.15267`, `-0.05358`, `-0.23223`, `-0.05358`; three finish only weakly positive or near zero (`avg_Mz_final≈0.01562`, `0.00161`, `0.000012`) and `0041` finishes slightly negative (`avg_Mz_final≈-0.00665`) | Step 1 therefore remains ambiguous for Branch B `hsfull`, and the safe interpretation is a family-level no-pulse ambiguity rather than a single anomalous run; do not generalize a pulse-necessity claim across all families without handling this branch explicitly | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup/run_status.json`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_B_followup_0030_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p01_jani0p0_damp0p5_twidth2500p0_hsfull/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_B_followup_0035_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p01_jani0p0_damp0p7_twidth2500p0_hsfull/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_B_followup_0041_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p01_jani0p0_damp0p4_twidth3000p0_hsfull/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-branchb-no-pulse-followup/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_B_followup_0043_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p01_jani0p0_damp0p7_twidth3000p0_hsfull/summary.json`
- `goal6-phase1-no-pulse-robustness-2026-04-15` | the four-case Goal 6 Phase 1 robustness batch does not close cleanly across all families | `goal6_A_main_0010`, `goal6_A_main_0059`, and `goal6_A_high_damping_0001` preserve positive magnetization without zero crossing (`avg_Mz_final≈0.99710`, `0.99836`, `0.99835`; `avg_Mz_min≈0.97687`, `0.98857`, `0.98969`), but `goal6_B_main_0042_nopulse...hsfull` reaches `avg_Mz_min≈-0.15267` and finishes at `avg_Mz_final≈-9.40e-4` even with `bias_amplitude=0` | this makes the Phase 1 gate mixed rather than clean, so any next claim should stay inside a narrow Branch B follow-up before general Step 2 mechanism interpretation | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-no-pulse-robustness/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/scan_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-15-goal6-no-pulse-robustness/run_status.json`
- `goal6-no-pulse-control-0003-2026-04-15` | the matched `no-pulse` replica of the strongest clean Goal 6 winner preserves a large electronic transient but does not switch magnetically | `goal6_A_main_0003_nopulse...` finished with `pulse_amp=0`, `avg_Mz_min≈0.98354`, `avg_Mz_final≈0.99040`, and `max_sigma_delta_abs≈0.17944`, essentially the same raw `sigma_delta` scale previously associated with the pulse-driven winner while the magnetization remains positive throughout | this validates the need for the planned reference subtraction `σ_pulse-σ_no_pulse` before assigning the large `sigma_delta` amplitude itself to the pulse-induced switching mechanism | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-14-goal6-no-pulse-controls/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_A_main_0003_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p3_twidth1000p0_hsneq/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-14-goal6-no-pulse-controls/results/matched_no_pulse_controls/goal6_A_main_0003_nopulse_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p3_twidth1000p0_hsneq/observables.tsv`
- `rhoeq-pade30-longtime-check-2026-04-15` | detached fixed-spin no-pulse `t_end=2000` Padé-pole comparison completed successfully and agrees with the validated baseline at the `~1e-7` level | `final_sigma_delta_absmax` changed only from `0.008869433401050286` (`N_λ2=20`) to `0.008869556791099631` (`N_λ2=30`), with `|Δ|=1.23390049344696e-07`; saved `rhoeq_maxabs_diff_vs_baseline=8.577397220580897e-08`, `sigma_eq_maxabs_diff_vs_baseline=5.1585239413398654e-08`, and `late10_sigma_delta_mean_diff_vs_baseline=1.2237989451859e-07` confirm that increasing `N_λ2` from `20` to `30` does not materially change the long-time residual in this setup | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-14-rhoeq-pade30-longtime-check/results/detached_20260415T004000Z/scan_summary.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-14-rhoeq-pade30-longtime-check/results/detached_20260415T004000Z/pade30_gso0p05_t2000/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-14-rhoeq-pade30-longtime-check/results/detached_20260415T004000Z/rhoeq_pade20_vs_pade30_t2000.png`
- `fixedllg_gso0p05-no-pulse-t2000-2026-04-13` | extending the same fixed-spin, no-pulse small-Rashba test from `t=1000` to `t=2000` reduces the residual substantially but still not to zero: `final_sigma_delta_absmax≈0.00887`, late-tail mean `≈0.00966`, and late-10% slope remains negative (`≈-8.15e-6`), so the signal is still decaying slowly rather than already pinned exactly at zero | this means the earlier `t=1000` residual was not a fully saturated plateau, but the system still has not reached zero within the tested horizon | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-13-rhoeq-longtime-relaxation/results/rhoeq_longtime_20260413T205653/pade20_gso0p05_t2000/summary.json`, `projects/2026-04-13-rhoeq-longtime-relaxation/results/rhoeq_longtime_20260413T205653/pade20_gso0p05_t2000/observables.tsv`
- `rhoeq-pade-convergence-2026-04-13` | for the same fixed-spin no-pulse small-Rashba setup used in the residual test, increasing the equilibrium Padé pole count from `N_λ2=20` to `30/40/60` changes `ρ_eq` only at the `~1e-7` level and `σ_eq` only at the `~6e-8` level, while `trace(ρ_eq)` stays at `4.0` within floating-point noise | this strongly indicates that the observed late residual `sigma_delta≈2.24e-2` is not caused by too few poles in `ρ_eq` at the current settings | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-13-rhoeq-pade-sweep/results/static_rhoeq_pade_20260413T204706Z/scan_summary.tsv`
- `fixedllg_gso0p05-no-pulse-2026-04-13` | in a no-pulse, fixed-spin, static-`s-d` test with Goal-5-like parameters (`Nx=Ny=2`, `j_sd=0.3`, `j_ex=-0.02`, `j_ani=0.03`, `gamma_so=0.05`), `max_site |sigma_delta_z_site|` decays from `~0.13384` to `~0.02242` by `t=1000`, with late-tail mean `~0.02366`, so it does not fully relax to zero on that timescale even for very small Rashba in this setup | artifacts: `projects/2026-04-13-fixed-llg-no-pulse-relaxation/results/fixed_llg_no_pulse_20260413T201501/fixedllg_gso0p05/observables.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-13-fixed-llg-no-pulse-relaxation/results/fixed_llg_no_pulse_20260413T201501/fixedllg_gso0p05/summary.json`
- `goal6-prepulse-sigma-delta-check-2026-04-13` | the finished Goal 6 results do not show `σ_delta = σ - σ_eq` relaxing near zero before `pulse_start=1000` | for representative winners `goal6_A_main_0003...hsneq`, `goal6_A_main_0034...hsneq`, and `goal6_A_main_0059...hsneq`, the late pre-pulse mean `max|σ_delta|` stays at `0.057-0.058` versus pre-pulse maxima `0.178-0.179`, i.e. about `32%` residual; the Branch B/center `hsfull` checks remain at `0.049-0.057` (`28-32%` residual) | `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_A_main_0003_pa1p5_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p3_twidth1000p0_hsneq/observables.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_A_main_0034_pa1p5_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p01_damp0p7_twidth2000p0_hsneq/observables.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_A_main_0059_pa1p5_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p7_twidth3000p0_hsneq/observables.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_B_main_0042_pa2p0_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p01_jani0p0_damp0p5_twidth3000p0_hsfull/observables.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_C_center_A_0002_pa1p5_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p3_twidth1500p0_hsfull/observables.tsv`
- `goal6-final-analysis-2026-04-13` | Goal 6 completed `117/117` traces with `6` strong in-pulse inversions and `20` strong retained finals. The best clean in-pulse retained switch is `goal6_A_main_0003...hsneq` (`pulse_window_min_avg_Mz≈-0.9205`, `avg_Mz_final≈-0.99974`), while the strongest delayed-retention case is `goal6_A_main_0034...hsneq` (`avg_Mz_final≈-0.99987` with weak in-pulse inversion). Branch B `hsfull` improves retention less effectively; its best case is `goal6_B_main_0042...` with `avg_Mz_final≈-0.96960`. Artifacts: `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/analysis/summary.md`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/analysis/goal6_in_pulse_ranked_runs.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/analysis/goal6_retention_ranked_runs.tsv`, `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/analysis/goal6_branch_summary.tsv`.

- `prepared-state-mitigation-check-2026-04-18` | the short validation cases were cloned from `projects/2026-04-10-goal6-retained-switch/results/queued_goal6_20260410T191626Z_first117/goal6_A_main_0003_pa1p5_gso0p9_jsd0p3_jexm0p02_jani0p03_damp0p3_twidth1000p0_hsneq/params.json`: `Nx=Ny=2`, `dt=0.1`, `gamma_so=0.9`, `j_sd=0.3`, `j_ex=-0.02`, `j_ani=0.03`, `damping=0.3`, `initial_configuration=FM`, `Hs_update_mode=nonequilibrium_only`, and right-lead-only bias geometry (`left_bias_scale=0`, `right_bias_scale=1`) | deliberate validation-only reductions were `t_end: 5000 -> 200`, `pulse_start: 1000 -> 100` for pulse cases, and `pulse_start=200`, `bias_amplitude=0` for no-pulse cases; the mitigation knobs introduced here are `equilibration_time=200` and `analysis_reference_mode=prepared_state`, while the longer follow-up used `equilibration_time=500` | `projects/2026-04-17-prepared-state-equilibration-check/run_prepared_state_comparison.py`, `projects/2026-04-17-prepared-state-equilibration-check/results/equilibration_time_sweep/prepared_nopulse_eq500/summary.json`

- `rhoeq-pade-convergence-2026-04-13` | the equilibrium-accuracy knob was chosen from code inspection: `ρ_eq(…, Np, …)` calls `pade_poles(Np)` directly in `../TDNEGF/src/stationary.jl`, so `N_λ2` is the direct control for the Padé quadrature used in `ρ_eq`; `N_λ1` was held at `49` because it belongs to the separate embedding/self-energy pole table loaded by `load_poles_square(N_λ1, N_λ2)` in `../TDNEGF/src/poles.jl` and then passed into `build_Σᴸ_nλ`, `build_Σᴳ_nλ`, and `build_χ_nλ` from `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl` | the test values `N_λ2 ∈ {20,30,40,60}` were chosen to bracket the current production default `20` and the legacy benchmark notebook choice `30`

- `hs-mode-prepulse-clarification-2026-04-13` | pre-pulse interpretation of `nonequilibrium_only` | the mode definition itself comes from `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/goal5.md`: `nonequilibrium_only` means the `H_s` drive uses `σ(t) - σ_eq`, while `full_spin_density` uses `σ(t)`. The repo-specific pre-pulse caveat comes from `projects/2026-04-08-tdnegf-spin-baseline/run_switching_case.jl` and `workflow_note.md`: electrons start from `u0 = zeros(...)`, the workflow explicitly says a zero initial state is used, and `ρ_eq(...)` is only computed as a reference during evolution, so pre-pulse `σ_delta` is expected to be ideally small only after transient relaxation, not guaranteed identically zero from `t=0`. | done

### Simulation uncertainty
- `prepared-state-mitigation-check-2026-04-18` | the new mitigation clearly suppresses the startup mismatch, but it does not prove exact coupled stationarity | `equilibration_time=200` lowers the Branch A-like short-run pre-pulse residual by about `10x`, yet the prepared-state floor remains finite at `~1.79e-2`, and a longer no-pulse preparation (`equilibration_time=500`) does not improve it materially (`~1.82e-2`) | confidence is moderate that pre-relaxation plus prepared-reference handling is the right practical fix for startup control and analysis, but low that it fully resolves the deeper enlarged-state fixed-point problem; a stricter fix would likely require a self-consistent coupled electron-spin stationary solve | open
- The pending direct-stationarity question is now resolved qualitatively: `ρ_eq(H_ab)` with zero auxiliaries is not a fixed point of the enlarged block TDNEGF solver in the tested zero-bias Branch A-like setup. What remains uncertain is the best corrective path: a self-consistent enlarged-state equilibrium initializer, a pre-relaxation stage before the pulse, or a reformulation of the reference observable against the true dynamical stationary state. Also unresolved is why the short `N_λ2=40` relaxations become nonfinite near `t≈7.7`; that numerical instability affects the late-time comparison but not the already measured `t=0` RHS mismatch.
- The available evidence does not support Padé pole truncation in `src/stationary.jl:120-143` as the dominant source of the pre-pulse `σ-σ_eq` residual: static `ρ_eq` changes are only `~10^-7` when increasing `N_λ2`, the direct `RHS(u_guess)` check is already clearly nonzero, and the residual shrinks materially with weaker SOC and longer no-pulse propagation. The main unresolved uncertainty is therefore no longer “whether there is a mismatch”, but how best to represent or initialize the correct dynamical stationary state of the enlarged solver.
- `brancha-rhoeq-pade-soc-check-2026-04-16` | this sweep intentionally used a fixed-spin, zero-bias no-pulse probe rather than the full coupled LLG evolution, so it isolates the electronic startup/reference mismatch more cleanly but does not by itself prove what the fully coupled pre-pulse `σ-σ_eq` would do after changing initialization or relaxation strategy | confidence is high that Padé convergence is not the dominant issue at `gamma_so=0.9` because `N_λ2=20/30/40` differ only at the `~10^-7` level in the final residual, but only moderate that `gamma_so` alone explains the full Branch A pre-pulse behavior because the true switching runs still evolve the local moments and recompute `ρ_eq(H_ab(t))` along a moving magnetic path | open
